# Supporting types

Generated by `bun run docs:protocol`. Edit source schemas, then regenerate.

JSON Schema validates structure. `x-ace-constraint` names additional semantic rules that clients must enforce; Zod remains the full validator.

## AccountAssignment

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountAssignment.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| instanceId | no | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| role | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":64} |
| estimatedLoad | yes | number | {"minimum":0,"maximum":100} |

Example:

```json
{
  "estimatedLoad": 9,
  "role": "example"
}
```

## AccountDirectory

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountDirectory.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Absolute Unix or drive-qualified Windows directory.

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"/example/home"
```

## AccountQuota

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountQuota.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| auth | yes | ["logged_in","logged_out","unknown"] |  |
| cursorSdkAuth | no | [CursorSdkAuth.json](schema/CursorSdkAuth.json) |  |
| observedAt | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| billingMode | no | ["api","subscription","unknown"] |  |
| plan | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| windows | yes | object | {"propertyNames":{"type":"string","maxLength":128},"additionalProperties":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/QuotaWindow.json"},"maxProperties":32,"x-ace-constraint":"At most 32 quota windows."} |
| blockers | no | object | {"default":{},"properties":{"overflow":{"type":"boolean","const":true},"limitError":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/QuotaWindow.json"}}} |
| usage | yes | object | {"properties":{"inputTokens":{"type":"number","minimum":0},"outputTokens":{"type":"number","minimum":0},"costUsd":{"type":"number","minimum":0}}} |

Example:

```json

  "auth": "logged_out",
  "observedAt": 2,
  "usage": {
    "costUsd": 4,
    "outputTokens": 2
  },
  "windows": {}
}
```

## AccountSummary

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountSummary.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| implicit | no | boolean |  |
| isDefault | no | boolean |  |
| id | yes | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| acpAgentId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| installationId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| instanceId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| homeStrategy | no | `"default_cli"` |  |
| isolation | no | `"unsupported"` |  |
| authMethod | no | ["browser","api_key","unknown"] |  |
| signedInAs | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| loginRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":128} |
| profileRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| installationVersion | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| provider | yes | [AccountProvider.json](schema/AccountProvider.json) |  |
| label | yes | string | {"maxLength":128} |
| quota | yes | [AccountQuota.json](schema/AccountQuota.json) |  |
| availability | yes | [AccountAvailability.json](schema/AccountAvailability.json) |  |
| blockedUntil | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"number","minimum":0},{"type":"null"}]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "availability": "logged_out",
  "blockedUntil": 2,
  "homeStrategy": "default_cli",
  "id": "example",
  "installationId": "example",
  "installationVersion": "example",
  "isolation": "unsupported",
  "label": "example",
  "loginRevision": "example",
  "provider": "opencode",
  "quota": {
    "auth": "logged_out",
    "observedAt": 2,
    "usage": {
      "outputTokens": 5
    },
    "windows": {}
  },
  "signedInAs": "example"
}
```

[JSON Schema](schema/AcpSessionSupport.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| capabilities | yes | object | {"properties":{"resume":{"type":"boolean"},"imageInput":{"type":"boolean"},"planMode":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["resume","imageInput","planMode"]} |
| mcp | yes | ["http","stdio","unavailable"] |  |
| modelSelection | yes | boolean |  |
| modeSelection | yes | boolean |  |
| subagentSessions | no | boolean | {"default":false} |

```json
    "imageInput": true,
    "planMode": false,
    "resume": true

  "modeSelection": false,
  "modelSelection": false,
  "raw": {
    "json": "example",
    "truncated": false
  },
  "visibility": "limited"
}
```

## AgentControlOperation

[JSON Schema](schema/AgentControlOperation.json), input validation.

### Variant 1

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"delegate_task"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128,"pattern":"^[a-zA-Z0-9_.-]+$"} |
| task | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":16384} |
| role | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":1024} |
| provider | yes | [ProviderKind.json](schema/ProviderKind.json) |  |
| permissionMode | no | [PermissionMode.json](schema/PermissionMode.json) |  |
| acpAgentId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| installationId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| instanceId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| model | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| accountId | no | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| options | no | [AgentLaunchOptions.json](schema/AgentLaunchOptions.json) |  |
| wait | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| estimatedLoad | no | number | {"default":0,"minimum":0,"maximum":100} |

Example:

```json
{
  "accountId": "example",
  "acpAgentId": "example",
  "estimatedLoad": 6,
  "installationId": "example",
  "op": "delegate_task",
  "options": {
```

[JSON Schema](schema/BrowserOrigin.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: An exact canonical HTTP(S) origin, including scheme and optional port, with no path, query, fragment or URL credentials.

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

```json
"https://youtube.com"
```

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"context.sampled"` |  |
| agentId | yes | [AgentId.json](schema/AgentId.json) |  |
| usedTokens | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| windowTokens | no | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| sessionId | no | string |  |
| model | no | string |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "agentId": "example",
  "type": "context.sampled",
  "usedTokens": 4
}
```

## CoreClientMessage

[JSON Schema](schema/CoreClientMessage.json), input validation.

### hello

Semantic rule: Exactly one of token and ticket is required.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"hello"` |  |
| channel | no | ["files","devices","browser","screen","provider_auth"] |  |
| protocolVersion | yes | `1` |  |
| deviceId | yes | [DeviceId.json](schema/DeviceId.json) |  |
| token | no | string |  |
| ticket | no | string |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "channel": "browser",
  "deviceId": "example",
  "protocolVersion": 1,
  "ticket": "example",
  "type": "hello"
}
```

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"subscribe"` |  |
| subscriptionId | yes | string | {"minLength":1} |
| scope | yes | [SubscriptionScope.json](schema/SubscriptionScope.json) |  |
| paced | no | boolean |  |
| afterSeq | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |

### snapshot

Semantic rule: seq must equal view.seq.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"snapshot"` |  |
| subscriptionId | yes | string |  |
| seq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| view | yes | [SnapshotView.json](schema/SnapshotView.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "seq": 9,
  "subscriptionId": "example",
  "type": "snapshot",
  "view": {
    "kind": "threads",
    "seq": 9,
    "threads": {}
  }
}
```

### events

Semantic rule: throughSeq >= afterSeq; events are ordered with (firstSeq ?? seq) > previous seq and seq <= throughSeq.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"events"` |  |
| subscriptionId | yes | string |  |
| afterSeq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| throughSeq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| events | yes | array | {"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/DeliveryEvent.json"}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "afterSeq": 3,
  "events": [],
  "subscriptionId": "example",
  "throughSeq": 9,
  "type": "events"
}
```

### progress

Semantic rule: throughSeq must be >= afterSeq.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"progress"` |  |
| subscriptionId | yes | string |  |
| afterSeq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| throughSeq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |

Example:

```json
{
  "afterSeq": 3,
  "subscriptionId": "example",
  "throughSeq": 9,
  "type": "progress"
}
```

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| workspace | no | [Project.json](schema/Project.json) |  |
| inspection | no | [ProjectInspection.json](schema/ProjectInspection.json) |  |
| threadId | no | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| commandId | yes | [CommandId.json](schema/CommandId.json) |  |
| ok | yes | boolean |  |
| forkThreadId | no | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| health | no | [DiagnosticsHealth.json](schema/DiagnosticsHealth.json) |  |
| error | no | string |  |
| alive | no | object | {"properties":{"agentsRunning":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"terminalsOpen":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"operationsRunning":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991}},"required":["agentsRunning","terminalsOpen","operationsRunning"]} |
| code | no | string |  |
| title | no | string |  |
| detail | no | string |  |
| review | no | [ReviewData.json](schema/ReviewData.json) |  |
| pr | no | [ForgePrRef.json](schema/ForgePrRef.json) |  |
| prStatus | no | [ForgePrStatus.json](schema/ForgePrStatus.json) |  |
| editor | no | [EditorLaunch.json](schema/EditorLaunch.json) |  |
| terminalId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| commit | no | string | {"pattern":"^[a-f0-9]{40,64}$"} |
| type | yes | `"commandResult"` |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "alive": {
    "agentsRunning": 9,
    "operationsRunning": 3,
    "terminalsOpen": 6
  },
  "commandId": "example",
  "forkThreadId": "example",
  "health": {
    "activeSessions": 9,
    "at": 1,
    "eventLoop": {
      "maxMs": 1,
      "meanMs": null,
      "p99Ms": null
    },
    "logs": {
      "dropped": 8,
      "failed": 6,
      "queued": 7
    },
    "memory": {
      "heapTotalBytes": 7,
      "heapUsedBytes": 1,
      "rssBytes": 0
    },
    "openHandles": 9,
    "queues": {},
    "sqlite": {
      "pageBytes": 4,
      "walBytes": 8
    }
  },
  "inspection": {
    "git": {
      "branch": "example",
      "defaultBranch": "example",
      "remotes": [],
      "root": "example"
    },
    "path": "example"
  },
  "ok": false,
  "title": "example",
  "type": "commandResult"
}
```

## CursorAuthEvent

### cursor.auth.login

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |

| loginId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| instanceId | yes | [AccountId.json](schema/AccountId.json) |  |
| state | yes | ["starting","browser","complete","failed","cancelled"] |  |
| expiresAt | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| url | no | intersection | {"allOf":[{"type":"string","format":"ace-whatwg-url","x-ace-url-maxLength":8192,"x-ace-constraint":"Accept strings whose trimmed value is an absolute WHATWG URL. Whitespace, Unicode hosts, opaque schemes and parser-normalized URLs are allowed. x-ace-url-minLength and x-ace-url-maxLength apply after trimming whitespace and removing ASCII tab, CR and LF characters, using UTF-16 code units. Install the ace-whatwg-url format validator or enforce this rule in application code."},{"type":"string","pattern":"^(?!.*\\s)https:\\/\\/\\S+$"}]} |

| error | no | ["login_failed","expired","cancelled"] |  |

| phase | yes | ["created","running","cancelling","settled"] |  |

| outcome | no | [DelegationOutcome.json](schema/DelegationOutcome.json) |  |
| resolvedModel | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| resultDelivery | no | ["parent","owner"] |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "childId": "example",
  "createdAt": 8,
  "depth": 6,
  "generation": 8,
  "parentAgentId": "example",
  "parentId": "example",
  "phase": "created",
  "request": {
    "accountId": "example",
    "model": "example",
    "options": {},
    "provider": "pi",
    "requestId": "Z_",
    "role": "example",
    "task": "example"
  },
  "requestId": "BcHCwD",
  "rootId": "example"
}
```

## DelegationRequest

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |

| task | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":16384} |
| role | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":1024} |
| provider | yes | [ProviderKind.json](schema/ProviderKind.json) |  |
| permissionMode | no | [PermissionMode.json](schema/PermissionMode.json) |  |
| acpAgentId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| installationId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| instanceId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| model | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| accountId | no | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| options | no | [AgentLaunchOptions.json](schema/AgentLaunchOptions.json) |  |
| wait | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| estimatedLoad | no | number | {"default":0,"minimum":0,"maximum":100} |

| id | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| name | yes | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| command | yes | string | {"maxLength":4096} |

Example:

```json
{
  "command": "example",
  "id": "example",
  "name": "example"
}
```

## Interaction

[JSON Schema](schema/Interaction.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | yes | [InteractionId.json](schema/InteractionId.json) |  |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| agentId | yes | [AgentId.json](schema/AgentId.json) |  |
| toolCallId | no | [ItemId.json](schema/ItemId.json) |  |
| blocking | yes | boolean |  |
| request | yes | [InteractionRequest.json](schema/InteractionRequest.json) |  |
| review | no | [PermissionReview.json](schema/PermissionReview.json) |  |
| state | yes | [InteractionState.json](schema/InteractionState.json) |  |
| expirationReason | no | `"provider_disconnected"` |  |
| resolution | no | [InteractionResolution.json](schema/InteractionResolution.json) |  |
| resolvedBy | no | [DeviceId.json](schema/DeviceId.json) |  |
| autoReviewed | no | boolean |  |
| createdAt | yes | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| closedAt | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| raw | no | array | {"default":[],"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/RawPayload.json"}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "agentId": "example",
  "blocking": true,
  "createdAt": 9,
  "id": "example",
  "raw": [],
  "request": {
    "kind": "approval",
    "mcpServer": {
      "name": "example",
      "source": "example"
    },
    "options": [],
    "permissionUpdates": [],
    "title": "example"
  },
  "state": "cancelled",
  "threadId": "example"
}
```

## InteractionEvidence

[JSON Schema](schema/InteractionEvidence.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| source | yes | ["screen-frames","browser-trace"] |  |
| target | yes | [InteractionTarget.json](schema/InteractionTarget.json) |  |
| action | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ScreenInput.json"},{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/BrowserInteractionAction.json"}]} |
| refreshHz | yes | number | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":240} |
| windowMs | yes | number | {"minimum":0,"maximum":10000} |
| latencyMs | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| longTasks | no | array | {"maxItems":100,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionGap.json"}} |
| layoutShift | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| hostLoad | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| captureOverheadPct | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| filmstrip | no | [InteractionFilmstrip.json](schema/InteractionFilmstrip.json) |  |
| updatesMs | yes | array | {"maxItems":2400,"items":{"type":"number","minimum":0,"maximum":10000}} |
| actionAtMs | no | number | {"minimum":0,"maximum":10000} |
| cores | no | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| truncated | no | boolean |  |
| refreshAssumed | no | boolean |  |
| notes | no | array | {"default":[],"maxItems":24,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":512}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "action": {
    "action": "type",
    "ref": "example",
    "text": "example"
  },
  "cores": 3,
  "latencyMs": 1,
  "layoutShift": 4,
  "refreshHz": 10,
  "source": "screen-frames",
  "target": {
    "kind": "browser-tab",
    "threadId": "example"
  },
  "updatesMs": [],
  "windowMs": 0
}
```

## InteractionMeasurement

[JSON Schema](schema/InteractionMeasurement.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| source | yes | ["screen-frames","browser-trace"] |  |
| target | yes | [InteractionTarget.json](schema/InteractionTarget.json) |  |
| action | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ScreenInput.json"},{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/BrowserInteractionAction.json"}]} |
| refreshHz | yes | number | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":240} |
| windowMs | yes | number | {"minimum":0,"maximum":10000} |
| latencyMs | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| settleMs | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| frames | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":2400} |
| effectiveFps | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| droppedFrames | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| hitches | yes | array | {"maxItems":20,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionGap.json"}} |
| hitchRatioMsPerS | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| hitchTimeMs | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| longTasks | no | array | {"maxItems":100,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionGap.json"}} |
| layoutShift | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| verdict | yes | [InteractionVerdict.json](schema/InteractionVerdict.json) |  |
| confidence | yes | [InteractionConfidence.json](schema/InteractionConfidence.json) |  |
| hostLoad | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| captureOverheadPct | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| filmstrip | no | [InteractionFilmstrip.json](schema/InteractionFilmstrip.json) |  |
| notes | yes | array | {"maxItems":32,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":512}} |
| repeat | no | [InteractionRepeat.json](schema/InteractionRepeat.json) |  |

Example:

```json

  "confidence": "medium",
  "filmstrip": {
    "data": "example",
    "mimeType": "image/jpeg",
    "type": "image"
  },
  "frames": 4,
  "hitches": [],
  "longTasks": [],
  "notes": [],
  "refreshHz": 10,
  "settleMs": 3,
  "source": "screen-frames",
  "target": {
    "bundleId": "example",
    "kind": "window",
    "windowId": 2
  },
  "verdict": "inconclusive",
  "windowMs": 7
}
```

## InteractionRepeat

[JSON Schema](schema/InteractionRepeat.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| runs | yes | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":5,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"verdict":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionVerdict.json"},"confidence":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionConfidence.json"}},"required":["verdict","confidence"]}} |
| metrics | yes | object | {"properties":{"refreshHz":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"windowMs":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"frames":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"latencyMs":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"settleMs":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"effectiveFps":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"droppedFrames":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"hitchRatioMsPerS":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"layoutShift":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"hostLoad":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"captureOverheadPct":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"longTaskMs":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"hitchMs":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"}},"required":["refreshHz","windowMs","frames","hitchMs"]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "metrics": {
    "effectiveFps": {
      "median": 7,
      "worst": 2
    },
    "frames": {
      "median": 2,
      "worst": 2
    },
    "hitchMs": {
      "median": 7,
      "worst": 5
    },
    "hitchRatioMsPerS": {
      "median": 0,
      "worst": 9
    },
    "hostLoad": {
      "median": 5,
      "worst": 2
    },
    "longTaskMs": {
      "median": 3,
      "worst": 3
    },
    "refreshHz": {
      "median": 8,
      "worst": 8
    },
    "settleMs": {
      "median": 2,
      "worst": 2
    },
    "windowMs": {
      "median": 6,
      "worst": 4
    }
  },
  "runs": [
    {
      "confidence": "low",
      "verdict": "no_change"
    }
  ]
}
```

```json
          "worst": 3
        },
        "windowMs": {
          "median": 7,
          "worst": 7
        }
      },
      "runs": [
        {
          "confidence": "high",
          "verdict": "no_change"
        }
      ]
    },
    "settleMs": 2,
    "source": "browser-trace",
    "target": {
      "kind": "browser-tab",
      "tabId": "example",
      "threadId": "example"
    },
    "verdict": "minor_hitches",
    "windowMs": 2
  },
  "type": "tool_call"
}
```

## ItemsWindowRequest

[JSON Schema](schema/ItemsWindowRequest.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Exactly one target; before + after + target is at most 200 items.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"items.window"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| aroundSeq | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| turnOrdinal | no | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| before | no | integer | {"default":50,"minimum":0,"maximum":199} |
| after | no | integer | {"default":50,"minimum":0,"maximum":199} |

Example:

```json
{
  "after": 8,
  "requestId": "example",
  "threadId": "example",
  "turnOrdinal": 2,
  "type": "items.window"
}
```

## ItemsWindowResponse

[JSON Schema](schema/ItemsWindowResponse.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"items.window"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| seq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| targetSeq | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]} |
| items | yes | array | {"maxItems":200,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/Item.json"}} |
| itemSeqs | yes | object | {"propertyNames":{"type":"string"},"additionalProperties":{"type":"integer","exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991}} |
| itemsBefore | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]} |
| itemsAfter | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "itemSeqs": {},
  "items": [],
  "itemsAfter": 3,
  "itemsBefore": null,
  "requestId": "example",
  "seq": 8,
  "targetSeq": null,
  "threadId": "example",
  "type": "items.window"
}
```

| result | yes | union | {"oneOf":[{"type":"object","properties":{"ok":{"type":"boolean","const":true},"nativeSessionId":{"type":"string"},"profile":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/PiProfile.json"}},"required":["ok"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"ok":{"type":"boolean","const":false},"error":{"type":"string","maxLength":1024}},"required":["ok","error"]}]} |

Example:

```json

    "ok": false
  },
  "type": "pi.result"
}
```

## PortableHandoff

[JSON Schema](schema/PortableHandoff.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| version | yes | `1` |  |
| sourceThreadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| origin | no | object | {"properties":{"provider":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProviderKind.json"},"backend":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256}},"required":["provider"]} |
| throughSeq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| lossy | yes | `true` |  |
| policy | yes | `"recent-complete-items"` |  |
| excerpts | yes | array | {"maxItems":200,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"citation":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/HandoffCitation.json"},"text":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}},"required":["citation","text"]}} |
| omittedItems | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| history | yes | object | {"properties":{"type":{"type":"string","const":"items.page"},"tool":{"default":"ace_read_handoff","type":"string","const":"ace_read_handoff"},"chunkTool":{"default":"ace_read_handoff_chunk","type":"string","const":"ace_read_handoff_chunk"},"threadId":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ThreadId.json"},"before":{"type":"integer","exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"limit":{"type":"number","const":50}},"required":["type","threadId","before","limit"]} |
| limitations | yes | `"Provider-private state is unavailable. Page history for omitted items, full text, reasoning, tools and attachments."` |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "excerpts": [],
  "history": {
    "before": 4,
    "chunkTool": "ace_read_handoff_chunk",
    "limit": 50,
    "threadId": "example",
    "type": "items.page"
  },
  "limitations": "Provider-private state is unavailable. Page history for omitted items, full text, reasoning, tools and attachments.",
  "lossy": true,
  "omittedItems": 8,
  "policy": "recent-complete-items",
  "sourceThreadId": "example",
  "throughSeq": 0,
  "version": 1
}
```

| env | no | object | {"default":{},"propertyNames":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":128},"additionalProperties":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}} |
| port | no | [PreviewPort.json](schema/PreviewPort.json) |  |

| url | no | string | {"format":"ace-whatwg-url","x-ace-url-maxLength":4096,"x-ace-constraint":"Accept strings whose trimmed value is an absolute WHATWG URL. Whitespace, Unicode hosts, opaque schemes and parser-normalized URLs are allowed. x-ace-url-minLength and x-ace-url-maxLength apply after trimming whitespace and removing ASCII tab, CR and LF characters, using UTF-16 code units. Install the ace-whatwg-url format validator or enforce this rule in application code."} |

```json
}
```

```json
}
```


Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.


### Variant 2

Type: number. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:


### Variant 3

Type: boolean. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
true
```

| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"provider.accounts.add"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| provider | yes | [NativeAccountProvider.json](schema/NativeAccountProvider.json) |  |
| label | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128,"pattern":"\\S"} |
| method | yes | [AccountLoginMethod.json](schema/AccountLoginMethod.json) |  |
| upstream | no | [ApiKeyUpstream.json](schema/ApiKeyUpstream.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "method": "api_key",
  "provider": "cursor",
  "requestId": "example",
  "type": "provider.accounts.add"
}
```

[JSON Schema](schema/ProviderConfiguration.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| provider | yes | [ProviderKind.json](schema/ProviderKind.json) |  |
| instance | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"} |
| enabled | no | boolean |  |
| defaultModel | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$","x-ace-constraint":"The pseudo model ID default is not accepted; choose a concrete model ID."},{"type":"null"}]} |
| binaryPath | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096,"pattern":"^(?:\\/\|[A-Za-z]:[\\\\/]).*$","x-ace-constraint":"Absolute executable path with no control bytes."} |
| customModels | no | array | {"maxItems":128,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"id":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"displayName":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"\\S"}},"required":["id","displayName"]},"x-ace-constraint":"Custom model IDs must be unique."} |
| hiddenModels | no | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"uniqueItems":true,"x-ace-constraint":"IDs must be unique."} |
| shownModels | no | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"uniqueItems":true,"x-ace-constraint":"IDs must be unique."} |
| hiddenGroups | no | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"uniqueItems":true,"x-ace-constraint":"IDs must be unique."} |
| shownGroups | no | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"uniqueItems":true,"x-ace-constraint":"IDs must be unique."} |
| favourites | no | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"uniqueItems":true,"x-ace-constraint":"IDs must be unique."} |
| showOnlyFavourites | no | boolean |  |
| hideDeprecated | no | boolean |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "defaultModel": null,
  "enabled": false,
  "hiddenGroups": [],
  "hiddenModels": [],
  "hideDeprecated": false,
  "provider": "opencode"
}
```

## ProviderInstance

[JSON Schema](schema/ProviderInstance.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Native instances use native account IDs. ACP instances require matching agent, installation and instance references, id equal to instanceId, default_cli home strategy and an empty selector environment.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | yes | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| implicit | no | `true` |  |
| managed | no | `true` |  |
| acpAgentId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| installationId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| instanceId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| homeStrategy | no | `"default_cli"` |  |
| authMethod | no | ["browser","api_key","unknown"] |  |
| signedInAs | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| loginRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":128} |
| profileRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| installationVersion | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| provider | yes | [AccountProvider.json](schema/AccountProvider.json) |  |
| label | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| homeDir | yes | [AccountDirectory.json](schema/AccountDirectory.json) |  |
| env | yes | object | {"propertyNames":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AccountEnvKey.json"},"additionalProperties":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AccountDirectory.json"}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "env": {
    "CODEX_HOME": "/example/codex"
  },
  "homeDir": "/example/codex",
  "id": "codex-default",
  "label": "Codex",
  "provider": "codex"
}
```

```json
  "type": "registry.bind",
  "version": "example"
}
```

```json
  "createdAt": 4,
  "id": "example",
  "source": {
    "from": {
      "kind": "commit",
      "ref": "example"
    },
    "to": {
      "id": "example",
      "kind": "checkpoint"
    },
    "workspaceId": "example"
  },
  "status": "changes-requested"
```
```json
}
```

## Run

[JSON Schema](schema/Run.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| permissionMode | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/PermissionMode.json"},{"type":"null"}]} |
| provider | no | [ProviderKind.json](schema/ProviderKind.json) |  |
| id | yes | [RunId.json](schema/RunId.json) |  |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| agentId | yes | [AgentId.json](schema/AgentId.json) |  |
| trigger | yes | [RunTrigger.json](schema/RunTrigger.json) |  |
| nativeId | no | string |  |
| ordinal | no | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| checkpoints | no | [RunCheckpoints.json](schema/RunCheckpoints.json) |  |
| executionSource | no | [ExecutionSource.json](schema/ExecutionSource.json) |  |
| state | yes | ["active","completed","interrupted","failed"] |  |
| startedAt | yes | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| endedAt | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| error | no | [AgentError.json](schema/AgentError.json) |  |

```json
  "agentId": "example",

  "threadId": "example",
  "trigger": "schedule"
}
```

## ScreenAction

[JSON Schema](schema/ScreenAction.json), input validation.

### click

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"click"` |  |
| x | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| y | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| button | no | ["left","right"] | {"default":"left"} |

Example:

```json
{
  "button": "right",
  "kind": "click",
  "x": 5,
  "y": 0
}
```

### type

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"type"` |  |
| text | yes | string | {"maxLength":4096} |

Example:

```json
{
  "kind": "type",
  "text": "example"
}
```

### key

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"key"` |  |
| keyCode | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":127} |
| modifiers | no | array | {"default":[],"maxItems":4,"items":{"type":"string","enum":["command","shift","option","control"]}} |

Example:

```json

  "keyCode": 9,
  "kind": "key"
}
```

### scroll

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"scroll"` |  |
| x | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| y | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| deltaX | yes | integer | {"minimum":-1000,"maximum":1000} |
| deltaY | yes | integer | {"minimum":-1000,"maximum":1000} |

Example:

```json
{
  "deltaX": 7,
  "deltaY": 3,
  "kind": "scroll",
  "x": 5,
  "y": 1
}
```

## ScreenCapabilities

[JSON Schema](schema/ScreenCapabilities.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| version | yes | `2` |  |
| background | no | boolean |  |
| windowSelection | no | boolean |  |
| permissionEvents | no | boolean |  |
| maxSessions | no | integer | {"minimum":1,"maximum":8} |
| platform | yes | ["macos","windows","linux-x11","linux-wayland"] |  |
| capture | yes | object | {"properties":{"windows":{"type":"boolean"},"displays":{"type":"boolean"},"changeDriven":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["windows","displays","changeDriven"]} |
| input | yes | object | {"properties":{"pointer":{"type":"boolean"},"keyboard":{"type":"boolean"},"scroll":{"type":"boolean"},"text":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["pointer","keyboard","scroll","text"]} |
| uiTree | yes | boolean |  |
| semanticActions | yes | array | {"maxItems":8,"items":{"type":"string","enum":["press","focus","setValue","scroll","expand","select","performSecondaryAction","selectText"]}} |
| codecs | yes | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":2,"items":{"type":"string","enum":["jpeg","h264"]}} |
| permissions | yes | object | {"properties":{"screen":{"type":"string","enum":["granted","denied","prompt","n/a"]},"input":{"type":"string","enum":["granted","denied","prompt","n/a"]}},"required":["screen","input"]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "background": false,
  "capture": {
    "changeDriven": true,
    "displays": false,
    "windows": true
  },
  "codecs": [
    "jpeg"
  ],
  "input": {
    "keyboard": false,
    "pointer": true,
    "scroll": false,
    "text": true
  },
  "permissionEvents": true,
  "permissions": {
    "input": "denied",
    "screen": "denied"
  },
  "platform": "linux-x11",
  "semanticActions": [],
  "uiTree": true,
  "version": 2,
  "windowSelection": false
}
```

## UsageQuery

[JSON Schema](schema/UsageQuery.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: to >= from, their difference is <= 365 days, and groupBy entries are unique.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| from | yes | string | {"format":"date","pattern":"^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]\|\\d\\d[13579][26]\|\\d\\d0[48]\|[02468][048]00\|[13579][26]00)-02-29\|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]\|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|3[01])\|(?:0[469]\|11)-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|30)\|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]\|1\\d\|2[0-8])))$"} |
| to | yes | string | {"format":"date","pattern":"^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]\|\\d\\d[13579][26]\|\\d\\d0[48]\|[02468][048]00\|[13579][26]00)-02-29\|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]\|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|3[01])\|(?:0[469]\|11)-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|30)\|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]\|1\\d\|2[0-8])))$"} |
| groupBy | no | array | {"default":[],"maxItems":7,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/UsageDimension.json"}} |
| filters | no | object | {"default":{},"propertyNames":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/UsageDimension.json"},"additionalProperties":{"minItems":1,"maxItems":50,"type":"array","items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":8192}}} |
| agentTree | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |
| limit | no | integer | {"default":100,"minimum":1,"maximum":1000} |
| orderBy | no | ["tokens","cost"] | {"default":"tokens"} |
| equivalentApiCost | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| bucket | no | ["day","week","month"] |  |
| quotaAccount | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |

Example:

```json
{
  "from": "2026-10-01",
  "to": "2026-10-02"
}
```

## WorktreeCreationEvent

[JSON Schema](schema/WorktreeCreationEvent.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |

| attempt | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| state | yes | ["running","cancelling","cancelled","failed","done","local"] |  |
| step | yes | ["preparing","fetching","creating","checking_out","setup","done"] |  |
| percent | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":100} |
| startedAt | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| elapsedMs | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| steps | yes | array | {"maxItems":6,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"step":{"type":"string","enum":["preparing","fetching","creating","checking_out","setup","done"]},"startedAt":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"elapsedMs":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991}},"required":["step","startedAt","elapsedMs"]}} |
| details | yes | array | {"maxItems":200,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":1024}} |
| message | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| cleanupComplete | yes | boolean |  |
| actions | yes | array | {"maxItems":3,"items":{"type":"string","enum":["cancel","local","retry"]}} |
| type | yes | `"worktree.creation.progress"` |  |

Example:

```json
  "actions": [],

  "startedAt": 8,
  "state": "cancelling",
  "step": "checking_out",
  "steps": [],
  "threadId": "example",
  "type": "worktree.creation.progress"
}
```
